- 04.10.26 06:36 [E-3304] [parent: E-3303] [T-212] [agent: saipen-cli] [op: root-cause-xterm-viewport-dispose] RUN: The one green run that failed (`T-212-packaged-green.json`, stage boot) is a real defect, not instrument noise. Full stack: `get dimensions` -> `t.Viewport.syncScrollArea` -> an anonymous callback at styles-C4MyjNav.js:22:44996, which is the Viewport constructor's own `setTimeout(()=>this.syncScrollArea())`. `RenderService.dimensions` is `return this._renderer.value!.dimensions`, and `_renderer` is a MutableDisposable that RenderService.dispose() empties, so any terminal built and disposed inside one macrotask throws `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'dimensions')` as an uncaught renderer exception. Upstream xterm has no disposal guard on this path (`Viewport` guards only `_smoothScroll`). The same exception is present on the pre-fix bundle (T-209-packaged-red-before-kill.json), so it is not a T-212 regression, but it is a shipped-app crash and it makes the oracle nondeterministic -- rerunning an unchanged green until three clean results is exactly the forbidden repeat.
